## `/en/blog/our-blog-1/platfus-picior-plat-cauze-simptome-si-tratament-eficient-6`

- Titlu: Platfus (Picior Plat): Cauze, Simptome și Tratament Eficient
- Descriere: Descoperă ce este platfusul (piciorul plat), care sunt principalele cauze, simptomele frecvente și cele mai eficiente metode de tratament, de la exerciții simple la soluții medicale avansate.

- 🖼 imagine-fundal: `/web/image/52429-afefdc25/Postare%20Blog%20%27Platfus%20%28picior%20plat%29%20-%20Cauze%2C%20simptome%20si%20tratament%20eficient%27%20cover%20image.webp` — alt: „” — arhivat: f65e69e581c91d0e0a84.webp

### Platfus (picior plat) - Cauze, simptome si tratament eficient

- 🔗 `#o_wblog_post_main`

dr. Gabriela Crișan - medic specialist reabilitare medicală | medic primar medicină sportiv April 28, 2025 by administrator blog

#### Ce este platfusul (piciorul plat)?

Piciorul plat, cunoscut și sub denumirea de platfus, este o afecțiune ortopedică în care bolta plantară este redusă sau absentă, ceea ce face ca întreaga talpă să vină în contact cu solul.

Un picior sănătos formează o curbă arcuită, iar oasele se formează în linie dreaptă. Un picior sănătos atinge podeaua în trei puncte - călcâiul, primul os metatarsian și cel de-al cincilea os metatarsian.

#### Cauzele apariției piciorului plat

##### Factori genetici

Unele persoane se nasc cu o predispoziție genetică spre platfus. Dacă unul dintre părinți are această afecțiune, există șanse mari ca și copilul să o moștenească.

##### Dezvoltarea insuficientă a bolții plantare

La sugari și copii mici, piciorul plat este normal, deoarece ligamentele și mușchii nu sunt încă suficient de dezvoltați. În mod normal, bolta plantară se formează până la vârsta de 6-7 ani, dar în unele cazuri acest proces nu are loc corespunzător. Copilul trebuie sa învețe mersul în casă desculț (ciorapi, șosete, botoșei). Utilizarea ghetelor ortopedice și a talonetelor la copiii de câteva luni, care nu merg, motivată că ar grăbi dezvoltarea boltei plantare este incorectă. Afară, încălțămintea trebuie să fie ușoară și cu talpa flexibilă pentru a da posibilitate plantei să ruleze pe sol în timpul mersului. Mersul se învață în medie între 9 și 12 luni, dar poate fi întârziat cu 1-2 luni de utilzarea premergătorului care favorizează hipotonia musculară.

- 🖼 imagine: `/web/image/52419-967ca228/Evolutia%20boltei%20plantare%20la%20copii.webp?access_token=76d6ba6f-0cfd-4e60-b6b9-a4d4274910e8` — alt: „” — arhivat: 2e0007d24c7b66786934.webp

##### Slăbirea mușchilor și ligamentelor

Este o insuficiență a mușchilor responsabili de menținerea curburii corecte a piciorului, cauzată, printre altele, de suprasolicitarea picioarelor prin munca în picioare, mersul prea des în pantofi incomozi, prea stramti, cu toc înalt, sau de purtarea pantofilor de proastă calitate sau de o inegalitate de lungime a membrelor inferioare.

##### Excesul de greutate corporala

Se pune o presiune suplimentară pe arcul piciorului, contribuind la aplatizarea lui.

##### Leziuni sau traumatisme

Loviturile, entorsele sau fracturile la nivelul piciorului pot afecta structurile care mențin bolta plantară, conducând la platfus.

##### Boli și afecțiuni asociate

Unele boli precum artrita reumatoidă, diabetul sau obezitatea pot contribui la dezvoltarea piciorului plat, prin afectarea ligamentelor și a structurilor osoase ale piciorului.

#### Simptomele piciorului plat

În multe cazuri, piciorul plat nu provoacă simptome evidente.

Totuși, în unele situatii apare discomfort sau durere în zona călcâiului, a labei piciorului, a gleznei sau a gambei după perioade lungi de mers sau stat în picioare; crampe , dureri musculare sau senzație de greutate în picioare, deformări ale piciorului (cum ar fi devierea călcâiului sau devierea degetelor de la picioare spre exterior), probleme de postură, care pot duce la dureri de spate, genunchi sau șold.

Platfusul poate afecta și distribuția greutății corpului, ceea ce poate duce la uzura inegală sau mai rapidă a pantofilor, în special pe o parte.

În plus, platfusul poate provoca transpirație excesivă a picioarelor, arsuri, mâncărime, micoză și tulburări circulatorii.

- 🖼 imagine: `/web/image/52422-a8b2a4d8/Discomfort%20si%20durere_simptome_platfus_picior%20plat.webp?access_token=9d969bcb-0d8d-481c-b9b8-b31defb22f39` — alt: „” — arhivat: be836c8b6bac2f983f22.webp

#### Metode de evaluare și diagnostic

##### Examen clinic

- trebuie efectuat de catre un medic instruit care observă forma piciorului și modul în care pacientul stă în picioare sau merge;

##### Testul urmei piciorului

- pacientul pășește pe o suprafață care evidențiază conturul tălpii;

- 🖼 imagine: `/web/image/52424-51f3ab31/Diagnostic%20platfus_urma%20piciorului.webp?access_token=5367df24-66a5-49e0-a997-6606ecf7a53f` — alt: „” — arhivat: 832c224b025c2b8b5f7e.webp

##### Radiografii sau RMN

- aceste investigații imagistice sunt necesare în cazurile severe sau atunci când se suspectează o problemă structurală a oaselor sau ligamentelor.

#### Tratamentul piciorului plat

##### Exerciții pentru întărirea mușchilor piciorului

Antrenamentele specifice, precum mersul desculț pe diferite suprafețe – atât plane, cât și pe iarbă sau alte suprafețe neuniforme – pentru a stimula mușchii să lucreze, mersul pe vârfuri, pe calcaie și părțile laterale ale picioarelor, mersul pe nisip sau rularea unei mingi sub talpă, automasajul, pot ajuta la menținerea unei bune funcționalități a piciorului. De asemenea, este recomandată purtarea de încălțăminte adecvată, menținerea igienei, menținerea unei greutăți corporale sănătoase și efectuarea de activități care susțin munca mușchilor din picioare.

- 🔗 `/en/kinetoterapie`

- 🖼 imagine: `/web/image/52423-e1a637c8/Exercitii_picior_plat.svg?access_token=16076f5e-76a2-49fa-8e3d-b100c6df522f` — alt: „” — arhivat: 46584a0afbb27a9e42a8.svg

##### Purtarea de încălțăminte adecvată

Este important ca pantofii să fie confortabili, cu susținere corespunzătoare pentru arcada piciorului.

- 🖼 imagine: `/web/image/52421-d832e5df/Incaltaminte_adecvata_platfus_picior%20plat.webp?access_token=2e5206e6-8439-47eb-bf5f-98f23692f127` — alt: „” — arhivat: 307ca4aebacaa80ad651.webp

##### Medicamente pentru durere și inflamație

În cazurile în care piciorul plat provoacă dureri severe, medicul poate recomanda antiinflamatoare nesteroidiene (AINS).

##### Utilizarea talonetelor ortopedice/branturi

Aceste dispozitive ortetice , deși nu vindecă afecțiunea în sine, pot fi eficiente în ameliorarea durerii cauzate de platfus prin susținerea bolții plantare și reducerea disconfortului în timpul mersului,

În stadiul avansat al bolii, dacă acest lucru nu aduce rezultate, este necesară intervenția chirurgicală.

- 🖼 imagine: `/web/image/52420-1880fdd6/Talonete_ortopedice_picior_plat_platfus.webp?access_token=a3f41e32-2cc0-43d7-b0d9-eda5b8acfdf8` — alt: „” — arhivat: 67c2eda33cc4d545fe28.webp

##### Terapia fizică - KINETOTERAPIA

Programul de kinetoterapie vizează cele mai eficiente exerciții pentru tonifierea musculaturii piciorului, creșterea elasticității tendoanelor, creșterea gradului de mobilitate și de stabilitate a articulției gleznei, îmbunătățirea echilibrului, corectarea posturilor greșite și reeducarea mersului.

- 🔗 `/en/kinetoterapie`

Află mai multe

- 🔗 `/en/electroterapie`

Se poate asocia Electroterapia pentru a stimula contractia muschilor slabiti, si a imbunatati circulatia sanguine locala.

Masajul terapeutic este de asemenea folosit pentru efectul antialgic, redarea tonusului, favorizarea drenajului limfatic și a circulației sangvine.

##### Intervenția chirurgicală

Când tratamentele conservatoare nu dau rezultate, poate fi necesară o operație pentru corectarea bolții plantare sau a altor probleme structurale ale piciorului.

De asemenea, este recomandată în cazul în care glezna este dureroasă la cele mai mici activități fizice, sau în cazul copiilor sau adolescenților care doresc să facă sport de performanță, pentru ca alinierea articulației subtalare va îmbunătăți parametrii fizici.

## `/en/blog/our-blog-1/redoarea-matinala-ce-ne-transmite-corpul-nostru-10`

- Titlu: Redoarea matinală - Ce ne transmite corpul nostru?
- Descriere: Redoarea matinală este senzația de „înțepenire”, dificultate la mișcare sau disconfort resimțit la primele mișcări după trezire. Pentru unii pacienți, termenul de „redoare” poate include durerea, oboseala, slăbiciunea sau limitarea mobilității. 

- 🖼 imagine-fundal: `/web/image/55446-d303dd5c/Postare%20Blog%20%27Redoarea%20matinal%C4%83%20-%20Ce%20ne%20transmite%20corpul%20nostru%3F%27%20cover%20image.webp` — alt: „” — arhivat: bec96a98e001f4f40769.webp

### Redoarea matinală - Ce ne transmite corpul nostru?

- 🔗 `#o_wblog_post_main`

December 10, 2025 by administrator blog

###### dr. Moldovan Kitti

medic specialist reumatolog

Dimineața, la primele mișcări, simțiți că articulațiile sunt ‘înțepenite’ și aveți nevoie de câteva minute până când reușiți să vă mișcați degetele sau genunchii normal?

Dacă vă regăsiți în această descriere, este posibil să fie vorba de ceea ce numim, în limbaj medical, redoare matinală.

Redoarea matinală este senzația de „înțepenire”, dificultate la mișcare sau disconfort resimțit la primele mișcări după trezire. Pentru unii pacienți, termenul de „redoare” poate include durerea, oboseala, slăbiciunea sau limitarea mobilității. În reumatologie, însă, redoarea este definită mai exact ca disconfortul și restricția articulației după o perioadă de inactivitate, fenomen cunoscut și sub numele de „fenomenul de gel” (tradus din engleză: „gel phenomenon”). Durata acestei senzații variază: redoarea ușoară poate ține doar câteva minute, în timp ce redoarea severă se poate prelungi ore întregi.

- 🖼 imagine: `/web/image/55445-ba76ec18/14190992_mono_7_man_woke_up_in_bed.webp?access_token=ab7be372-10c6-4712-b19d-a66411ad96a7` — alt: „” — arhivat: efc3957787b829590a40.webp

#### Cauzele redorii matinale

În cazul bolilor degenerative, precum artroza, cartilajul articular se subțiază, iar articulația își pierde elasticitatea. Redoarea asociată este de obicei scurtă, de câteva minute până la aproximativ 20, și se agravează după efort, ameliorându-se la repaus.

În schimb, redoarea de tip inflamator, întâlnită în artrite inflamatorii precum artrita reumatoidă sau polimialgia reumatică, poate dura mai multe zeci de minute sau chiar câteva ore. Aceasta este adesea însoțită de umflături, căldură locală și durere accentuată în repaus, uneori chiar cu treziri nocturne.

Este important de reținut că durata și caracterul redorii oferă indicii despre tipul afecțiunii: redoarea scurtă, dependentă de efort, sugerează un proces degenerativ, iar redoarea prelungită, prezentă mai ales dimineața și ameliorată de mișcare, indică un proces inflamator.

Alte cauze posibile includ tensiunea musculară, posturile neadecvate de somn sau perioadele lungi de inactivitate.

#### Când este necesar un consult reumatologic?

Majoritatea cazurilor de redoare matinală ușoară, ocazională, care durează doar câteva minute și se ameliorează cu mișcarea, schimbarea vremii sau după câteva zile, nu necesită îngrijorare imediată. De exemplu, dacă redoarea apare după un efort fizic intens și dispare în 3–5 zile, aceasta este adesea o reacție normală a organismului.

Totuși, există situații în care este indicat să solicitați o evaluare reumatologică. Este recomandat să consultați un specialist dacă:

- Redoarea persistă mai mult de 30–60 de minute dimineața, apare în mai multe zile la rând sau se însoțește de durere care nu se ameliorează cu repaus. De asemenea, implicarea mai multor articulații simultan, episoadele repetate de înțepenire, tumefierea, roșeața, căldura locală sau limitarea vizibilă a mișcării sunt semne care trebuie investigate.

- Durerea care apare fără legătură cu efortul fizic, care trezește pacientul noaptea sau care persistă mai mult de două săptămâni, precum și asocierea cu simptome generale – febră, oboseală, scădere în greutate sau erupții cutanate – justifică de asemenea evaluarea.

- Sunteți diagnosticat cu psoriazis, întrucât aproximativ 30–40% dintre pacienți pot dezvolta artrită psoriazică..

Consultul precoce poate ajuta la identificarea corectă a cauzei redorii, la prevenirea complicațiilor și la inițierea timpurie a tratamentului, care poate ameliora simptomele și proteja articulațiile pe termen lung.

#### Cum putem preveni apariția durerii?

Stilul de viață are un impact direct asupra sănătății articulațiilor. Deși unele afecțiuni reumatologice au o componentă genetică sau pot apărea în contexte pe care nu le putem controla, numeroși factori care influențează inflamația și uzura articulară țin de alegerile noastre zilnice. Stresul, sedentarismul, excesul ponderal sau suprasolicitarea articulațiilor pot agrava durerea, iar evitarea lor poate reduce riscul de a dezvolta artrită sau de a intensifica simptomele existente.

Mișcarea regulată are un rol protector, deoarece îmbunătățește lubrifierea articulațiilor, reduce inflamația și întărește musculatura care susține scheletul. O postură corectă, o alimentație echilibrată și evitarea factorilor nocivi – precum fumatul – contribuie la menținerea unor articulații sănătoase pe termen lung.

De asemenea, alegerea unor activități cu impact redus, purtarea echipamentului de protecție adecvat atunci când este necesar și respectarea limitelor propriului corp ajută la prevenirea traumatismelor care pot grăbi deteriorarea articulațiilor.

#### Cum arată un stil de viață corect pentru sănătatea articulațiilor?

1. Mișcare regulată

Cel puțin 30 de minute de activitate fizică moderată, de minimum trei ori pe săptămână. Exercițiile cu impact redus – mers alert, înot, ciclism – sunt ideale pentru a menține articulațiile mobile fără a le suprasolicita.

2. Greutate corporală echilibrată

Excesul ponderal crește presiunea asupra genunchilor, șoldurilor și coloanei. Chiar și 5 kilograme în plus pot dubla riscul de gonartroză.

3. Postură corectă și ergonomie

O poziție corectă la birou și în activitățile zilnice reduce suprasolicitarea articulațiilor și previne apariția durerilor musculo-scheletale.

4. Gestionarea stresului

Somnul adecvat, tehnicile de relaxare și pauzele active scad nivelul hormonilor de stres, care pot agrava inflamația.

5. Alimentație echilibrată

O dietă variată, bogată în nutrienți antiinflamatori, ajută la menținerea greutății optime și la reducerea inflamației sistemice.

6. Renunțarea la fumat

Produsele din tutun cresc inflamația și accelerează deteriorarea țesuturilor. Evitarea lor reduce semnificativ riscul de apariție a durerilor articulare și a bolilor reumatologice.

7. Protejarea articulațiilor

În activitățile care pot implica traumatisme, purtarea echipamentului de protecție adecvat și evitarea mișcărilor repetitive sau forțate ajută la prevenirea leziunilor articulare.

#### Ce facem când durerea a apărut deja?

Primii pași până ajungem la medic

Dacă durerea este recentă și nu există semne de alarmă, putem încerca:

Aplicarea locală de comprese calde sau reci: gheață în primele 48 de ore dacă durerea a apărut după efort, sau căldură dacă durerea este musculară sau cronică.

Reducerea activităților dureroase, dar fără imobilizare totală. Mișcarea blândă menține articulația funcțională.

Aplicarea locală a cremelor antiinflamatoare sau antialgice, care sunt în general sigure pentru majoritatea pacienților.

Corectarea poziției și exerciții ușoare de mobilizare. Întinderi blânde, fără durere, pot reduce redoarea articulară.

În plus, la debutul durerii, putem aplica metoda RICE pentru a obține un efect rapid de calmare și reducere a inflamației.

Rest – Repaus

Ice – Gheață

Compression – Compresie

Elevation – Elevare

Dacă este sigur pentru dumneavoastră, se pot folosi și creme antiinflamatoare sau antialgice, aplicate local. De asemenea, este util să notăm detalii despre durere – localizare, intensitate, ce o agravează sau ce o ameliorează –, informații care îl vor ajuta pe medic.

Dacă simptomele persistă, se agravează sau apar tumefieri, febră, erupții cutanate sau scădere în greutate, este necesară o evaluare reumatologică.

#### Concluzie

Redoarea matinală este un simptom important. Poate fi o reacție normală după repaus, dar dacă durează mai mult sau se repetă constant, aceasta poate să ascundă procese inflamatorii sau degenerative. Identificarea precoce și consultul reumatologic pot preveni deteriorarea articulațiilor și pot îmbunătăți calitatea vieții pe termen lung.

Bibliografie:

Informațiile din acest articol se bazează pe recomandările societăților de reumatologie și pe ghiduri internaționale actuale.

- 🔗 `https://www.rheumatology.org/patient-blog/when-to-see-a-rheumatologist`

- American College of Rheumatology, 2025. rheumatology.org

- Kelley and Firestein's Textbook of Rheumatology, 10th edition, 2017.

## `/en/blog/our-blog-1/scoliosis-what-it-is-how-it-manifests-and-what-treatment-options-are-available-4`

- Titlu: Scolioza: Ce este, cum se manifestă și ce opțiuni de tratament există
- Descriere: Scolioza reprezintă cea mai frecventă tulburare de statică vertebrală în plan frontal. Reprezintă o curbură anormală a coloanei vertebrale spre lateral și este una din cele mai comune afecțiuni la copii și adolescenți.

- 🖼 imagine-fundal: `/web/image/52081-7c3e9a27/Postare%20Blog%20%27Scolioza%27%20cover%20image.webp` — alt: „” — arhivat: a4adcc4920a58bfdea54.webp

### Scoliosis - What it is, how it manifests, and what treatment options are available

- 🔗 `#o_wblog_post_main`

March 31, 2025 by administrator blog

###### dr. Székely Margit

physician specializing in physical rehabilitation

The spine is the central component of the human musculoskeletal system. It must ensure an optimal balance between stability and mobility so that it can support the body’s weight while allowing for an adequate range of motion necessary for efficient biomechanical function. As an adaptation to the upright posture, the spine has four physiological curves in the sagittal plane that increase its strength, allow for shock absorption during locomotion, and facilitate its mobility. The normal spine is straight in the frontal plane.

- 🖼 imagine: `/web/image/52041-3885db4a/Picture1.jpg?access_token=9ddec004-7069-4a26-b8cc-7caf87ef74de` — alt: „” — arhivat: f3f55451029228e8394e.jpg

Scoliosis is the most common disorder of spinal alignment in the frontal plane. It is an abnormal lateral curvature of the spine and is one of the most common conditions in children and adolescents. It can be classified into two main types: functional and structural.

In functional, nonstructural scoliosis, the anatomy of the vertebrae and discs is normal; only the alignment in the frontal plane is disrupted. The lateral deviation of the spine is completely reducible, both clinically and radiographically, in the supine position.

In structural scoliosis, the vertebrae exhibit structural changes (rotations, wedge-shaped deformities). The lateral deviation of the spine is clinically and radiologically irreducible in the supine position. Idiopathic scoliosis is the most common form of structural scoliosis. The lateral deviation of the spine occurs in a healthy child, with no exact cause being identifiable.

From an etiological perspective, functional scoliosis includes scoliotic postures (without an obvious cause), reducible scoliotic curves (which are usually antalgic postures associated with spinal conditions), and compensatory curves resulting from certain conditions (e.g., lower limb length discrepancy). Structural scoliosis is most often idiopathic, with no known cause.

For the diagnosis of scoliosis, the most important step is the clinical examination, which allows for the identification of the characteristic signs of spinal curvature. This examination must be performed with the patient undressed, wearing only briefs. The entire body must be examined while the patient is standing, viewed from the front, in profile, and from the back. Attention should be paid to the symmetry of the shoulders and shoulder blades; the symmetry of the waist triangles formed between the trunk and the upper limbs hanging freely at the sides of the body; the symmetry of the chest; rib deformities; and the symmetry of the pelvis.

Scoliosis can be detected by placing a plumb line on the occipital protuberance or the C7 spinous process. This test can determine whether the scoliosis is balanced or not. If the lead weight starting from the C7 vertebra falls into the intervertebral fold, the scoliosis is balanced, with the C7 and S1 spinous processes aligned. If the plumb line falls outside the intervertebral fold, the scoliosis is unbalanced. The thoracic and/or lumbar curvature can be measured using a scoliometer.

Scoliosis can be detected by placing a plumb line on the occipital protuberance or the C7 spinous process. This test can determine whether the scoliosis is balanced or not. If the lead weight starting from the C7 vertebra falls into the intervertebral fold, the scoliosis is balanced, with the C7 and S1 spinous processes aligned. If the plumb line falls outside the intervertebral fold, the scoliosis is unbalanced. The thoracic and/or lumbar curvature can be measured using a scoliometer.

With regard to the treatment of idiopathic scoliosis, there are three therapeutic approaches: physical therapy, orthopedic treatment with braces, or surgical treatment. Physical therapy is an integral part of all treatment programs, whether used alone in cases of non-progressive scoliosis with a Cobb angle of less than 30°, or in conjunction with orthopedic or surgical treatment. Orthopedic treatment is indicated for scoliosis exceeding 30° in patients with skeletal immaturity, or for scoliosis around 20° that is progressive (worsening by 6° between two follow-up examinations conducted 6 months apart). Surgical treatment is indicated for cases in which the Cobb angle exceeds 40–45°.

The treatment plan must be tailored to each individual case, taking into account not only the specific medical circumstances but also the family’s circumstances and the feasibility of adhering to the treatment regimen. It must be continuously adjusted based on the results obtained; therefore, medical checkups are required every 6 months throughout the growth period, and annually after skeletal maturity is reached.

The progression of scoliosis without treatment depends on its severity and type, but in general it can lead to a progressive worsening of the spinal curvature, especially during periods of rapid growth, such as puberty. Without intervention, scoliosis can have the following consequences: progressive increase in the angle of curvature, chronic pain, noticeable body asymmetries, pulmonary and cardiovascular complications, limited spinal mobility, and a negative impact on quality of life.

For the early diagnosis of scoliosis, it is essential that every parent carefully observe their child’s back and recognize the characteristics of this condition, that all children be examined periodically by their family doctor and/or school physician, and that screening programs be implemented for early detection. If scoliosis is suspected, a consultation with a specialist is required, during which the diagnosis is established based on a clinical examination and diagnostic tests, after which an appropriate treatment will be recommended.

Bibliography

1. Iconia Borza – Patologia aparatului locomotor – noțiuni de ortopedie și traumatologie, Editura Mirton, Timișoara, 2006

2. Dinu M. Antonescu – Patologia aparatului locomotor, vol II, Editura Medicală, București, 2005-2008

## `/en/blog/our-blog-1/what-does-an-internist-actually-do-5`

- Titlu: Ce face, de fapt, medicul internist? | SEVALIA Targu Mures
- Descriere: Află ce face un medic internist și de ce este considerat „arhitectul sănătății tale”. Descoperă cum un consult de medicină internă îți poate oferi un plan personalizat pentru o sănătate echilibrată pe termen lung.

- 🖼 imagine-fundal: `/web/image/52195-1648d81d/Postare%20Blog%20%27Ce%20face%2C%20de%20fapt%2C%20medicul%20internist%3F%27%20cover%20image.webp` — alt: „” — arhivat: 85bcb0a5252b2fcc83ba.webp

### What does an internist actually do?

- 🔗 `#o_wblog_post_main`

April 8, 2025 by administrator blog

Many patients come in for their first internal medicine consultation without knowing exactly what this specialty entails. The question “What does an internist actually do?” is one of the most common—and rightly so.

An internist is not just a “general practitioner,” as many people believe. He or she is the specialist who sees the big picture of your health and carefully and precisely develops a personalized plan for you. Just as an architect has a vision for an entire house, an internist has a vision for your health as a whole.

### What is internal medicine?

Internal medicine is a complex branch of medicine that deals with the prevention, diagnosis, and treatment of diseases of the internal organs—from the heart and lungs to the digestive system, kidneys, and endocrine system.

Internists often treat chronic conditions or symptoms that are difficult to diagnose, as they are the ones who integrate information from various medical fields and look for connections between seemingly unrelated symptoms.

It is, in essence, your starting point on a coherent and well-coordinated medical journey.

### What exactly does an internist do?

● listens carefully,

● analyzes the data,

● correlates the information,

● draws up a plan,

● track changes over time.

Comprehensive evaluation

An internal medicine consultation begins with a detailed medical history—a discussion of your medical history, current symptoms, lifestyle, and family history. It’s not just a simple question of “what’s wrong with you,” but a comprehensive investigation that looks for patterns and connections.

Correlation of symptoms

Do you feel constantly tired, have abdominal pain, and experience slight fluctuations in your blood pressure?

The internist will try to determine whether there is an underlying cause—a metabolic disorder, an endocrine problem, or perhaps side effects from a treatment.

Recommendation for appropriate investigations

Instead of having tests done on your own, at random, your internist will recommend the specific tests you need: blood tests, ultrasounds, EKGs, and additional consultations. Not too few, not too many — just what’s relevant for you.

Treatment coordination

If you already have multiple health conditions (such as diabetes, high blood pressure, and digestive problems), your internist will make sure that your treatments do not “conflict” with one another.

He is the one who sees the whole clinical picture and brings it all together.

long-term follow-up

An internist doesn't just treat isolated episodes. He or she monitors your progress over time, adjusts your treatment plan based on your body's response, and prevents complications.

### When should you see an internist?

● When you have multiple symptoms and don't know where to start

● If you have multiple chronic conditions (e.g., high blood pressure + diabetes + hyperlipidemia)

● For comprehensive annual checkups, even if you don't have any symptoms

● When you want a second opinion or an integrated treatment plan

● If you want prevention, not just treatment

- 🔗 `/en/medicina-interna-consultatii-tirgu-mures`

Do you need a clear picture of your health? Find out how an internist can help you

### Why do we say it’s the “architect of your health”?

Imagine that your health is a building. It could be a solid, well-maintained structure, or one with hidden problems in its foundation.

An internist thinks long-term.

It doesn't just address what's visible on the surface; it looks for the real causes.

It doesn't react to each symptom individually, but rather develops a strategy.

He constantly reviews the plan, adjusts it, and guides you.

This is the person who knows you best from a medical standpoint and who becomes your partner on this journey—not just “a temporary specialist.”

An internist is the best specialist to turn to when you need a clear, comprehensive, and personalized medical assessment. They are the architect who helps you build lasting, balanced, and well-protected health for the long term.

Don't let vague symptoms hold you back. A consultation with an internist can provide you with answers and guidance.

## `/en/blog/our-blog-1/why-do-you-have-a-headache-when-is-it-normal-and-when-should-you-see-a-neurologist-12`

- Titlu: De ce te doare capul? Când e cazul să mergi la un neurolog?
- Descriere: Când este normală durerea de cap și când trebuie să mergi la neurolog? Află cauzele, semnele de alarmă și când e cazul să te îngrijorezi.

- 🖼 imagine-fundal: `/web/image/57159-13cc6c7a/Postare%20Blog%20%27De%20ce%20te%20doare%20capul%3F%20C%C3%A2nd%20e%20normal%20%C8%99i%20c%C3%A2nd%20ar%20trebui%20s%C4%83%20mergi%20la%20neurolog%3F%27%20cover%20image.webp` — alt: „” — arhivat: 353254a4866f2cd8088f.webp

### Why do you have a headache? When is it normal, and when should you see a neurologist?

- 🔗 `#o_wblog_post_main`

April 27, 2026 by administrator blog

###### Dr. Emese Orban

neurologist

#### Introduction

Headaches are among the most common and debilitating neurological conditions worldwide. The International Classification of Diseases defines headaches as painful conditions affecting the upper part of the head that can severely impair daily functioning and quality of life. (1)

Headaches can severely impact patients’ lives, leading to increased absenteeism from work and school, as well as substantial economic losses due to reduced productivity and healthcare costs (2, 3). The onset and progression of various types of headaches can be attributed to a biopsychosocial model, which includes biological (obesity, sleep disturbances), psychological (stress, psychiatric disorders), and social determinants (4, 5). A significant proportion of these factors can be modified, which may reduce the occurrence of headaches. Therefore, understanding the burden and trends of headaches is essential for public health planning, healthcare delivery, and research priorities. (6)

- 🖼 imagine: `/web/image/57194-4c646ca7/modern-technology-job-people-concept-portrait-tired-young-female-employee-with-hair-bun-taking-off-eyeglasses-massaging-her-nose-bridge-feeling-stressed-because-lot-work.jpg?access_token=f10aa8ae-4b49-4ef1-804e-ee45bff1dc19` — alt: „Femeie lucrand cu o durere de cap (cefalee)” — arhivat: 2df8fb17b0fee28745be.jpg

#### Warning signs

Most headaches are benign, caused by stress, dehydration, or fatigue. However, certain types may signal serious neurological conditions, and recognizing the warning signs associated with headaches can save lives (7). Specific symptoms that require urgent medical attention include:

sudden, intense pain (“the worst headache of my life”)

changes in familiar headache patterns (more frequent, more intense, or different from usual)

headaches accompanied by fever, confusion, altered consciousness, weakness, numbness, or problems with vision, speech, or balance

headache associated with a traumatic brain injury

new headaches after age 50

headache accompanied by persistent vomiting, especially in the morning

headaches in people with known medical conditions: cancer, HIV/immunosuppression, bleeding disorders

pain triggered by physical exertion, coughing, sneezing, or sexual intercourse

Recognizing warning signs helps distinguish harmless pain from dangerous conditions. Any of these findings in the context of a headache should prompt a neurological evaluation and further testing.

#### Types of headaches

Certain types of headaches are more commonly encountered in primary care. These include migraine, which occurs episodically in 12–16% of the general population, predominantly among women. It is characterized by attacks of moderate or severe headache, often unilateral in location, with a throbbing quality, lasting between 4 and 72 hours, frequently associated with nausea and/or vomiting, and is aggravated by routine physical activity. Another major subtype is migraine with aura, which affects 10% of the population and is characterized by symptoms such as visual disturbances, unilateral paresthesias, or dysphasia that develop gradually and resolve within approximately 60 minutes.

The second most common type of headache encountered in clinical practice is the tension-type headache, which lasts from a few hours to several days, is usually generalized, and is typically described as a feeling of pressure or tightness, like a band wrapped tightly around the head. This type of headache occurs episodically and affects over 80% of the population.

Cluster headaches are easy to recognize; they primarily affect men and are strictly one-sided, occurring around the eye.

- 🖼 imagine: `/web/image/57195-82a894d1/tired-man-after-very-long-day.jpg?access_token=bb59d63d-e943-412c-8685-24967be90084` — alt: „” — arhivat: 35e439b19db87b296a76.jpg

It is short-lived (15–18 minutes), occurs frequently, and is characterized by the presence of autonomic symptoms such as tearing, runny nose, nasal congestion, and ptosis (drooping eyelid). This type of headache causes marked agitation; the patient is unable to stay in bed and feels a constant need to move around.

A common type of chronic headache is medication-overuse headache, which occurs almost daily and is associated with excessive use of pain relievers. This type of headache is most often more severe in the morning upon waking.

#### Triggering factors

Beyond the type of pain itself, triggering factors play an important role. Among the most common are stress, lack of sleep or an irregular sleep schedule, severe fatigue, and dehydration. Diet can also influence the onset of headaches: skipping meals, alcohol consumption, excessive caffeine intake, or, conversely, abruptly stopping coffee consumption can trigger painful episodes. Some people are sensitive to certain foods, such as chocolate, aged cheeses, or related products.

Common environmental factors that contribute to headaches include bright light, loud noise, strong odors, or changes in the weather. For some people, headaches can be influenced by hormonal fluctuations, such as during menstruation. Postural factors should not be overlooked either; for example, prolonged sitting at a desk in front of a computer can contribute to the onset of pain.

#### Common mistakes in managing headaches

Many patients try to manage the problem on their own, which can lead to complications: frequent use of pain relievers without a doctor’s recommendation, delaying a specialist consultation, and ignoring recurring patterns of pain. In some cases, overuse of medication can even make the headache worse.

Treatment depends on the type and cause of the headache. It may include: lifestyle changes (sleep, hydration, stress reduction), medication tailored to each patient, and further testing when necessary.

#### Conclusions

In conclusion, headache is one of the most common complaints encountered in medical practice, with a wide range of causes, from benign primary forms to potentially serious secondary causes. Correctly identifying the type of headache is essential for appropriate treatment.

In this context, the neurological examination plays a central role in the evaluation of patients with headache. It allows for the differentiation between primary and secondary forms by identifying any focal neurological signs, changes in mental status, or other clinical clues suggestive of an underlying condition. A normal neurological examination, in the absence of red flags, most often supports a benign diagnosis, reducing the need for costly or invasive additional investigations.

On the other hand, the presence of abnormalities on neurological examination necessitates further investigation, sometimes on an emergency basis, to rule out serious conditions such as intracerebral hemorrhages, space-occupying lesions, or central nervous system infections. Thus, the clinical examination remains a first-line tool, indispensable in the triage and management of patients with headache.

Therefore, careful integration of the patient’s medical history with the results of the neurological examination significantly contributes to establishing an accurate diagnosis, guiding diagnostic tests, and initiating appropriate treatment, thereby having a direct impact on the patient’s prognosis and quality of life.

- 🖼 imagine: `/web/image/57196-5bcced4f/midsection-doctor-showing-x-ray-patient-hospital.jpg?access_token=a0d7ac5e-5b0c-4d2f-9cfe-d6d9a1e4fc1a` — alt: „” — arhivat: bacf5dc5072bb2de407f.jpg

## `/en/decompresie-vertebrala-robotizata`

- Titlu: SEVALIA | Decompresie vertebrala robotizata | Mures
- Descriere: ratament avansat care utilizează tehnologie robotizată pentru a elibera presiunea asupra discurilor intervertebrale și a ameliora durerile de spate.

- 🖼 imagine-fundal: `/web/image/51628-f0074fc6/IMG-106.webp` — alt: „” — arhivat: 8fea7352f01b93f72d45.webp

### ROBOTIC SPINAL DECOMPRESSION

- 🖼 imagine-fundal: `/web/image/51627-f0074fc6/IMG-106.webp` — alt: „” — arhivat: 8fea7352f01b93f72d45.webp

### ROBOTIC SPINAL DECOMPRESSION

- 🔗 `#`

###### The therapy uses automatic decompressive forces to mobilize the joints, reduce pressure on neurospinal structures, relax and elongate soft tissues using pain-relieving positions, which can be adjusted in countless combinations depending on the patient's condition.

###### It is a non-invasive treatment for acute and chronic sciatica pain triggered by a herniated or bulging disc in the lower back or spinal stenosis.

###### The procedure targets the root cause of the pain and uses a repeated, controlled and consistent traction force.

###### Spinal decompression procedures are performed only on the recommendation of a specialist following a radiologic examination or MRI scan, under the strict supervision of a physiotherapist.

##### Chronic lower back pain

##### Herniated disk

##### Spondylosis

##### Chronic neck pain

##### Scoliosis

##### Reduces disc pressure

- 🖼 imagine: `/web/image/39070-c11c5ee8/Decompresie%20vertebrala_relaxeaza%20musculatura%20spatelui.webp` — alt: „” — arhivat: 9b8f475c74641e331ac7.webp

- 🖼 imagine: `/web/image/39071-556faf46/Decompresie%20vertebrala_amelioreaza%20compresia%20radacinii%20nervoase.webp` — alt: „” — arhivat: 28c4068372986877587b.webp

- 🔗 `/en/contactus`

Book an appointment

### Non-invasive alternative to spinal surgery

Spinal Decompression is the first option to reduce back pain of any origin regardless of its stage. The therapy is on the borderline between advanced manual techniques, a non-invasive alternative to spinal surgery and drug-free back pain management. The therapy uses automatic decompressive forces to mobilize the joints, reduce pressure on neuro-spinal structures, relax and elongate soft tissues using pain-relieving positions that can be adjusted in countless combinations depending on the patient's condition.

##### RECOMMENDATIONS

The main indications for robotic spinal decompression are:

For subacute or chronic lumbar and cervical pain with a discal (disc spurs or herniated discs), musculoligamentous or articular cause.

Certain forms of herniated cervical and lumbar discs.

For structural, paralytic, neuropathic or idiopathic scoliosis.

For kyphosis (represents increased convexity of the thoracic spine).

For ankylosing spondylitis.

Vertebral osteoconrosis (Scheurmann's disease).

Piaget disease

Spina bifida.

##### CONTRAINDICATIONS

Robotic spinal decompression is not recommended for patients with the following conditions:

Osteoporosis;

Malignant tumors of the spine;

Infections of the spine and spinal discs;

Spinal fractures;

Severe kyphoscoliosis;

Inflammatory spinal diseases:

Insuficiență cardiacă;

Heart failure;

Aneurysm;

Heart rhythm disturbances;

Carotid or vertebral artery disease;

Pregnant patients;

Abdominal conditions.

Contraindications are not absolute.

A specialist medical consultation and doctor's approval is essential.

## `/en/drenaj-limfatic`

- Titlu: Drenaj limfatic | Stimularea circulatiei limfatice
- Descriere: Drenaj limfatic cu aparat BTL pentru edeme, varice, celulită și detoxifiere. Tehnologie avansată, eficiență fără durere, adaptată nevoilor tale.

- 🖼 imagine-fundal: `/web/image/52968-8eef2387/DSC05983.webp` — alt: „” — arhivat: a19bb80d1d2472d90baf.webp

### LYMPHATIC DRAINAGE

- 🖼 imagine-fundal: `/web/image/52969-8eef2387/DSC05983.webp` — alt: „” — arhivat: a19bb80d1d2472d90baf.webp

### LYMPHATIC DRAINAGE

In addition to manual lymphatic drainage, we also offer the possibility of 100% lymphatic drainage with medical devices.

BTL lymphatic drainage is a modern form of pressotherapy, performed with a medical device that applies controlled sequential pressure to the limbs. This pressure stimulates lymphatic circulation, helps eliminate toxins and reduces inflammation and fluid retention.

Unlike manual drainage, the BTL machine procedure is more precise, hygienic and fully customizable. Pressure, duration and treated areas are digitally controlled according to the patient's needs. The compression suit applied to the limbs has inflatable chambers that work in sequences, mimicking the natural action of the lymphatic system.

It is indicated both in medical conditions (lymphedema, incipient varicose veins, post-surgical edema) and for aesthetic purposes (anti-cellulite, slight body remodeling). Lymphatic drainage with BTL is also recommended during periods of recovery, helping to reduce pain and improve general well-being.

#### Primary and secondary lymphoedema

#### Venous insufficiency and fluid retention

#### Post-operative and post-traumatic edema

#### Heavy legs, early varicose veins

#### Cellulite and lymphatic circulation disorders

- 🔗 `/en/contactus`

Book an appointment

### Lymphatic drainage - Therapy for edema, detoxification and recovery

Lymphatic drainage with BTL equipment is a non-invasive, computer-controlled therapy that stimulates the lymphatic system through rhythmic pneumatic pressure. The procedure is designed to accelerate the elimination of toxins, reduce edema and inflammation and support the body's natural regeneration process.

##### RECOMMENDATIONS

The main indications of lymphatic drainage are:

Lymphedema, postoperative or post-traumatic edema

Uncomplicated varicose veins, venous insufficiency

Heavy legs, poor circulation

Cellulite, water retention, detoxification

Post-surgical recovery

##### CONTRAINDICATIONS

The procedure is not recommended for patients with the following conditions:

Active deep vein thrombosis

Severe heart failure

Pregnancy (abdominal areas)

Local infections or severe dermatitis

Active neoplasms without oncologic opinion

Contraindications are not absolute.

A specialist medical consultation and doctor's approval is essential.

### Frequently asked questions about lymphatic drainage

- 🔗 `#`

What is BTL lymphatic drainage?

It is a computerized form of pressotherapy that uses sequential pressure to stimulate lymphatic circulation and reduce edema.

- 🔗 `#`

Is the procedure painful?

No. It is completely painless and relaxing. Many patients describe it as a gentle, deeply relaxing massage.

- 🔗 `#`

Does it help cellulite and detox?

Yes, lymphatic drainage with BTL is effective in reducing water retention, improving skin appearance and fighting cellulite.

- 🔗 `#`

Can I have lymphatic drainage if I have varicose veins?

If they are in mild or medium stage and there are no medical contraindications, yes. However, an initial assessment is needed.

- 🔗 `#`

Can I have lymphatic drainage after surgery?

Yes, it is often recommended for reducing edema and speeding up the postoperative healing process.

## `/en/dus-subacval`

- Titlu: Dus subacval | Terapie Hidrokinetică pentru Relaxare și Recuperare
- Descriere: Dușul subacval este o procedură de recuperare ce relaxează mușchii, ameliorează durerea și stimulează circulația. Eficient în reumatism și stres cronic.

- 🖼 imagine-fundal: `/web/image/52957-7019bd0c/DSC05751.webp` — alt: „” — arhivat: c1a6d5e4ebd009f88465.webp

### DUS SUBACVAL

- 🖼 imagine-fundal: `/web/image/52958-7019bd0c/DSC05751.webp` — alt: „” — arhivat: c1a6d5e4ebd009f88465.webp

### ELECTROTHERAPY

Dușul subacval este o tehnică de hidroterapie ce constă în aplicarea unui jet de apă sub presiune direct pe corpul pacientului, în timp ce acesta este scufundat într-o cadă cu apă caldă (35–37°C). Procedura combină efectele termice ale apei cu efectul mecanic al jetului, având rezultate excelente în relaxarea musculară, activarea circulației și reducerea durerilor musculo-articulare.

Jetul este direcționat manual de către un fizioterapeut, în funcție de zonele cu probleme, intensitatea presiunii fiind ajustată în funcție de toleranța pacientului. Această terapie are și un efect tonic general, contribuind la reducerea nivelului de stres și îmbunătățirea stării de bine.

Dușul subacval este recomandat atât în afecțiuni cronice – precum reumatismul degenerativ sau tulburările circulatorii –, cât și în stări posttraumatice sau pentru pacienți suprasolicitați fizic și psihic. Este o metodă blândă, dar profund eficientă, care poate fi integrată într-un program de recuperare complex.

#### Dureri

#### lombare cronice

#### Artroze (genunchi, șold, coloană)

#### Contracturi musculare persistente

#### Tulburări circulatorii periferice

#### Stres cronic

- 🖼 imagine: `/web/image/39073-3fb2e3e7/Decompresie%20vertebrala_imbunatateste%20fluxul%20sanguin.webp` — alt: „” — arhivat: 88b10222ecaa2f2c2e40.webp

##### Stimularea circulației sanguine și limfatice

- 🔗 `/en/contactus`

﻿Book an appointment﻿

### Terapie hidrokinetică pentru relaxare și recuperare

Duș subacval - Terapie modernă ce combină presiunea apei cu mișcarea controlată pentru efecte terapeutice profunde

Utilizat frecvent în cadrul terapiilor de recuperare medicală, reumatologie și relaxare musculară, dușul subacval are efecte deosebite asupra tonusului muscular, circulației periferice și mobilității articulare. La clinica noastră, această procedură este aplicată de personal specializat, într-un mediu sigur și confortabil, adaptat nevoilor individuale ale fiecărui pacient.

##### RECOMMENDATIONS

Principalele indicații ale dușului subacval sunt:

Dureri de spate, tensiuni musculare cronice

Reumatism degenerativ (artroză)

Recuperare post-operatorie ortopedică

Stres, anxietate, tulburări de somn

Circulație periferică deficitară

##### CONTRAINDICATIONS

Nu este recomandată procedura pentru pacienții cu următoarele afecțiuni:

Afecțiuni dermatologice active (eczeme, infecții)

Tromboflebite sau tulburări de coagulare

Hipertensiune arterială necontrolată

Sarcină avansată

Tumori maligne sau stări febrile

Contraindicațiile nu sunt cu caracter absolut.

A specialist medical consultation and doctor's approval is essential.

### Întrebări frecvente despre dușul subacval

- 🔗 `#`

Ce presupune exact dușul subacval ?

Procedura constă în aplicarea unui jet de apă sub presiune pe corp, în timp ce pacientul este în apă caldă, sub supravegherea unui terapeut.

- 🔗 `#`

Este o procedură dureroasă?

Nu. Este o procedură non-invazivă, relaxantă, iar presiunea jetului este adaptată permanent toleranței pacientului.

- 🔗 `#`

Pot face duș subacval în paralel cu kinetoterapia?

Da. Este chiar recomandat să fie combinat cu alte terapii pentru un efect sinergic în procesul de recuperare.

- 🔗 `#`

Pot beneficia de duș subacval dacă am varice ?

Da, însă cu presiune redusă și doar dacă varicele nu sunt într-un stadiu avansat sau complicat. Evaluarea medicului este esențială.

- 🔗 `#`

Este nevoie de o evaluare medicală înainte?

Da, un consult medical este necesar pentru a verifica indicațiile și a exclude eventualele contraindicații.

- 🔗 `#`

Se poate face și în scop de relaxare, nu doar medical?

Absolut. Dușul subacval este foarte eficient și pentru relaxare musculară, combaterea stresului și îmbunătățirea calității somnului.

## `/en/electroterapie`

- Titlu: SEVALIA | ELECTROTERAPIE | MURES
- Descriere: Tratament avansat care utilizează curenți electrici pentru a reduce durerea și a accelera recuperarea musculară și articulară.

- 🖼 imagine-fundal: `/web/image/3801-c56d5547/DSC04452.webp` — alt: „” — arhivat: 54a550a15ca1b4ad72dd.webp

### ELECTRTHERAPY

- 🔗 `#`

- 🖼 imagine-fundal: `/web/image/3801-c56d5547/DSC04452.webp` — alt: „” — arhivat: 54a550a15ca1b4ad72dd.webp

### ELECTROTHERAPY

#### What you need to know

###### Adjuvant treatment

Electrotherapy is a component of physiotherapy which, in combination with other methods, helps in several disorders of the muscular system.

###### Electrical impulse stimulation

The principle of electrostimulation is to stimulate nerve fibers to combat pain and malfunction of one or more muscle groups.

###### Non-invasive therapy

There is no discomfort during the procedure and the associated risks are minimal and extremely rare.

#### Effects of electrotherapy

###### Increased local metabolism and tissue diffusion

###### Effective in working muscle mass without cardiovascular or mental effort

###### Vasodilatory, excitatory, relaxing, decongestive, nutritive, resorptive and analgesic thermal effects

##### RECOMMENDATION

The main indications of electrotherapy are:

Post-traumatic conditions - such as contusions, sprains or dislocations;

Muscular overstretch and strains;

Peripheral circulatory disorders (e.g. varicose disease, vascular alteration caused by frostbite or burns);

In the case of certain conditions of the osteoarticular system (e.g. spinal damage in scoliosis);

Connective tissue diseases (e.g. scleroderma);

For rheumatologic conditions such as degenerative rheumatism, inflammatory (chronic) rheumatism or rheumatoid arthritis;

In the case of recent fractures, delayed callus formation or hematoma;

For the treatment of neurological disorders (e.g. neuralgia, neuritis, but also neuralgic sequelae following herpes simplex or herpes zoster).

Increase, tone, relax and recover muscle mass;

Lymphatic drainage or capillarization (bursitis, synovial cysts);

Neuralgic and degenerative (arthritic) conditions.

##### CONTRAINDICATION

Electrotherapy is not recommended for the following categories of patients:

Patients with pacemakers;

Patients with arrhythmias and heart disease;

Those suffering from epileptic seizures;

Those with open wounds or various skin pathologies;

On the site of surgery more recent than 6 months;

Patients with tumor formations;

Pregnant women;

Patients with inguinal and abdominal hernia.

The contraindications are not absolute.

A specialist medical consultation and doctor's approval is essential.

- 🔗 `#`

#### TRABERT CURRENTS

They are low-frequency currents that are characterized by rectangular-shaped pulses and have an analgesic and hyperemic trophic effect.

Trabert current treatment helps to reduce pain, stimulate blood circulation, improve muscle function, reduce muscle spasms, promote tissue healing and last but not least, control inflammation.

The Trabert current procedure is recommended for:

musculoskeletal disorders (contusions, sprains, strains, sprains, muscle strains, etc.)

rheumatic articular and abarticular disorders (reactivated **or relapsing** arthrosis, arthritis, stiloiditis, epicondylitis, scapulohumeral periarthritis)

neurological disorders (neuralgia, neuritis)

peripheral circulatory diseases

- 🔗 `#`

#### TENS (TRANSCUTANEOUS ELECTRICAL NERVE STIMULATION)

TENS current therapy helps manage pain by using a mild electrical current. As the name suggests, this type of therapy involves electrically stimulating nerves, modulating the painful pain signals sent to the brain.

TENS therapy therefore helps combat various painful conditions, such as:

arthritis

lower back pain

pain from hard and repetitive work

pain caused by nerve damage

sports injuries

- 🔗 `#`

#### DIADYNAMIC CURRENT (DDC)

They belong to the category of low-frequency alternating current therapies.

Treatment with diadynamic currents helps to reduce pain, stimulate blood circulation, reduce inflammation, promote tissue healing and last but not least both muscle relaxation and stimulate muscle contraction, depending on the condition being treated.

There are several forms of diadynamic currents, their use depending on the desired effect. The main forms include:

Monophasic Diadynamic Currents

Currents with constant polarity, which can have varying frequencies.

They have an excitatory effect on motor nerves (excitomotor effect).

They can be used to treat chronic pain.

Biphasic Diadynamic Currents

They have alternating polarity and can have different frequencies.

They have a strong analgesic (pain-relieving) effect.

They are often used to treat acute pain.

Interferential Diadynamic Currents

Involve two sinusoidal currents with close frequencies that meet in the treated tissue, generating interferential currents.

They are used to treat muscle and joint pain.

Pulsed Diadynamic Currents

Pulse at regular intervals and can have analgesic effects.

They can be used to treat chronic pain and to stimulate blood circulation.

Continuous Diadynamic Currents

Currents with a constant polarity that can have analgesic and antispasmodic effects.

They can be used to treat chronic pain and muscle spasms.

- 🔗 `#`

#### INTERFERENTIAL CURRENT (ICT)

Interferential current therapy utilizes two medium-frequency currents, which enter the tissues simultaneously and cross. This interaction gives rise to an interferential current that has the characteristics of a low-frequency current (in other words, the interference of the two currents mimics low-frequency stimulation).

The 4 main applications of interferential currents are pain reduction, muscle stimulation, increased local blood flow and edema reduction.

Interferential currents have the following effects on the human body:

Analgesic effect

Stimulate muscle contraction

Vasodilatation of blood vessels

Improved nutritional and tissue metabolizing processes

Impact on the autonomic nervous system

- 🔗 `#`

#### ELECTRICAL STIMULATIO

Electrical stimulation is a common technique used in physiotherapy, involving the application of electrical current to treat various musculoskeletal and neurological conditions. It can be performed using different types of devices and treatment protocols, and its benefits may vary depending on the condition being treated and the individual characteristics of the patient.

Therapeutic indications:

Electrical stimulation can be used to treat a wide range of conditions, including chronic pain, post-surgical recovery, muscle atrophy, sports injuries, neurological conditions (such as stroke or neuropathy), and many others.

Treatment protocols may vary depending on the specific condition and therapeutic goals of each patient.

Benefits of electrical stimulation in physiotherapy:

Pain relief: Electrical stimulation can block the transmission of pain signals to the brain, providing pain relief in chronic or acute conditions.

Improve blood circulation: Electric current can stimulate vasodilation and thus increase blood flow to the treated area, facilitating the transportation of nutrients and oxygen and the removal of metabolic waste products.

Improve muscle function: Electrical stimulation can be used to stimulate muscle contractions, facilitating the maintenance and growth of muscle mass, improving muscle tone and preventing atrophy.

- 🖼 imagine-fundal: `/web/image/3795-864cba8e/DSC04470.webp` — alt: „” — arhivat: b1263e4a82ed9c0a7e57.webp

##### INNOVATIVE THERAPY

Equipped with the most advanced devices and equipment in the field, we ensure you a quality medical act that meets the most demanding requirements.

- 🔗 `#`

SHOCKWAVE THERAPY

It is the first choice for treating chronic pain of the musculoskeletal system, calcifications and tendon disorders or non-union fractures. ShockWave therapy uses focused electro-acoustic waves that cause deep cellular stimulation.

SHOCKWAVE therapy procedure is recommended if:

suffer from joint or tendon pain

you're not having surgery

you are looking for a non-invasive treatment alternative without drugs or local anesthetics.

- 🔗 `/en/shockwave-focusat`

More details

- 🔗 `#`

SUPER INDUCTIVE SYSTEM (SIS)

The innovative SUPER INDUCTIVE INDUCTIVE SYSTEM (SIS) therapy is based on a high intensity electromagnetic field which has positive effects on the tissue. The therapeutic effects of this therapy are pain relief, fracture healing, myorelaxation (relaxation of the muscular system), myostimulation (stimulation of the muscular system) and joint mobilization.

The SUPER INDUCTIVE SYSTEM (SIS) therapy procedure is recommended if you want to:

reduce a muscle spasm

consolidați musculatura

heal a fracture

recovering after a stroke

- 🔗 `/en/super-inductive-system-sis`

More details

- 🔗 `#`

TR-Therapy

TECAR (Capacitive and Resistive Energy Transfer) therapy stimulates the body's self-healing mechanisms by radiofrequency currents that penetrate the tissue and transform into heat. The selective thermal enhancement of tissue causes effects such as pain relief, myorelaxation, increased local blood circulation and reduction of edema.

The procedure is recommended in the treatment of acute and chronic musculoskeletal disorders such as:

muscle spasms

degenerative joint diseases

back pain

soft tissue injuries.

- 🔗 `/en/tr-therapy`

More details

## `/en/emsella-tratament-pentru-incontinenta-urinara`

- Titlu: EMSELLA | Terapia planșeului pelvin
- Descriere: —

- 🖼 imagine-fundal: `/web/image/6368-41c60295/EMSELLA_REBEca.webp` — alt: „” — arhivat: 197b3abce5f4a057685e.webp

### EMSELLA - Pelvic floor therapy

- 🖼 imagine-fundal: `/web/image/51632-143f1094/IMG_2459.webp` — alt: „” — arhivat: 1f37597c680e7d46c0c1.webp

### EMSELLA

- 🔗 `#`

###### EMSELLA therapy is indicated in the treatment of urinary incontinence, acting by strengthening the pelvic floor muscles. It works thanks to the patented HIFEM (High-Intensity Focused Electromagnetic Technology) system - an extremely powerful focused electromagnetic field whose intensity allows the patient to achieve supra-maximal muscle contractions while sitting comfortably on the EMSELLA applicator-potolol.

###### The EMSELLA device uses non-invasive electromagnetic energy and increases pelvic floor muscle tone. This regains bladder control and reduces incontinence episodes.

###### - A single session with this technology can induce the equivalent of 11,000 Kegel exercises in 28 minutes, making it much more effective than many other methods of strengthening the pelvic floor muscles.

###### 95% of treated patients reported a significant improvement in quality of life.

##### Urinary incontinence

##### Vaginal laxity

##### Perineal rupture

##### Chronic pelvic pain

##### Repeated cystitis

##### Sexual dysfunctions

###### Who is it recommended for?

EMSELLA is ideal for women of any age who need a solution for urinary incontinence and improved quality of life.

###### How does the procedure feel?

During the procedure you will feel tingling and twitching of the pelvic muscles, but nothing painful. You can resume your daily activities immediately after the treatment.

###### How long does the treatment take?

One session lasts 28 minutes. Most patients need 6 sessions scheduled twice a week. But a prior consultation is necessary to create a personalized schedule.

###### How quickly can you see results?

Improvements can be seen from the very first session and the results will continue to improve throughout the treatment and in the following weeks.

##### Do questions and physical changes overwhelm you?

We are your support during this time.

- 🔗 `/en/contactus`

Let's talk

#### What is needed before the procedure?

Annual check-ups within the last 12 months. Women need a Pap smear and men need a PSA (prostate-specific antigen) test.

It also requires a recently performed urinalysis and uroculture.

#### How do we monitor results?

BLADDER CONTROL ASSESSMENT QUESTIONNAIRE

Together with the doctor you will fill out the questionnaire to see your bladder control score and we will customize your treatment plan.

URINATION CALENDAR

Once your treatment plan is started, we will leave you a calendar that you will fill in daily to monitor the progress of your treatment plan. Based on your doctor's observations, the plan will be tailored and personalized to your progress.

- 🖼 imagine: `/web/image/5507-75109160/BEFORE.webp` — alt: „” — arhivat: 1dc374e23cf53b106069.webp

#### BEFORE

The pelvic floor muscles do not sufficiently support the pelvic organs and affect bladder control

Loss of neuromuscular control

- 🖼 imagine: `/web/image/5506-0380ad0b/AFTER.webp` — alt: „” — arhivat: 3a78b147f056f029a1e2.webp

#### AFTER

Stimulation helps to regain control of the pelvic muscles and bladder.

##### RECOMMENDATION

The main indications of pelvic floor therapy with EMSELLA are:

Patients with symptoms of urinary incontinence

Prolapse grade 1-2

Sexual dysfunction

Vaginal laxity

Perineal rupture

Chronic pelvic pain

Repeated cystitis

##### CONTRAINDICATION

For the following categories of patients the EMSELLA procedure is not recommended:

Pregnant women

During your period;

Patients with electronic or metallic implants (copper sterile)

Patients with pacemakers;

Patients with tumor formations;

Patients with implanted defibrillators, implanted neurostimulators;

Contraindications are not absolute.

A specialist medical consultation and doctor's approval is essential.

- 🖼 imagine: `/web/image/5623-d656a897/surgery_3481661.webp` — alt: „” — arhivat: 60b8b6fb7ba95a477b34.webp

##### Without surgery

The procedure is completely non-invasive, so no surgery is required.

- 🖼 imagine: `/web/image/5698-7abe9ad1/abdominal_5730044.webp` — alt: „” — arhivat: 6e4f40826079e1d4cc97.webp

##### Pain-free and effortless

The procedure is one that allows you to remain fully clothed and relaxed throughout the treatment, while the electro-magnetic pulses perform the contractions.

- 🖼 imagine: `/web/image/5624-f3c12b72/medicine_4494278.webp` — alt: „” — arhivat: 08004784077e9db8eec6.webp

##### Without medication

The EMSELLA procedure does not require medication before or after therapeutic sessions.

- 🖼 imagine: `/web/image/5625-4ee4f05f/cycle_4494272.webp` — alt: „” — arhivat: 4ad3d739afbe4fcce72e.webp

##### No recovery period

No rest or recovery period is required after EMSELLA sessions, so you can resume your daily activities immediately after treatment.

- Titlu: Fizioterapie Tîrgu Mureș | SEVALIA

- 🖼 imagine-fundal: `/web/image/3801-c56d5547/DSC04452.webp` — alt: „” — arhivat: 54a550a15ca1b4ad72dd.webp

### FIZIOTERAPIE

- 🖼 imagine-fundal: `/web/image/3801-c56d5547/DSC04452.webp` — alt: „” — arhivat: 54a550a15ca1b4ad72dd.webp

### FIZIOTERAPIE

###### Fizioterapia este o disciplină medicală care ajută la restabilirea mișcării și funcționalității corpului afectat de boli, leziuni sau dizabilități. Prin tehnici moderne și exerciții specifice, fizioterapia contribuie la ameliorarea durerii, îmbunătățirea mobilității și recuperarea generală a pacienților.

###### Dacă ai nevoie de fizioterapie în Mureș, clinica noastră îți oferă tratamente personalizate pentru o gamă largă de afecțiuni.

#### Beneficiile fizioterapiei

###### Reducerea durerii

prin tehnici de electroterapie, ultrasunete, terapie manuală.

###### Îmbunătățirea mobilității și flexibilității

exerciții specifice pentru recuperarea mișcărilor pierdute.

###### Prevenirea complicațiilor

programe personalizate pentru menținerea sănătății.

###### Creșterea forței musculare

prin exerciții terapeutice adaptate fiecărui pacient.

###### Reeducarea posturală

pentru corectarea poziției corporale și prevenirea durerilor cronice.

- 🖼 imagine: `/web/image/51739-0a1dee24/_DSC3554.webp` — alt: „” — arhivat: 2602cf5eba4684c4a52a.webp

##### Kinetoterapie

Exerciții specifice pentru îmbunătățirea mobilității și forței musculare.

- 🔗 `/en/kinetoterapie`

﻿Află mai multe﻿

##### Electroterapie

Utilizarea curenților electrici pentru refacerea funcției musculare și reducerea durerii.

- 🔗 `/en/electroterapie`

﻿Află mai multe﻿

- 🖼 imagine: `/web/image/51741-e159f34a/_DSC3604.webp` — alt: „” — arhivat: ffaa323575252136eafb.webp

##### Hidroterapie

Terapie prin apă pentru relaxare și refacerea articulațiilor.

### Întrebări frecvente despre fizioterapie

- 🔗 `#`

Ce afecțiuni sunt tratate prin fizioterapie?

###### Afecțiuni ortopedice

Exemple: entorse, luxații, hernie de disc, fracturi, scolioză.

Tratament: mobilizări, exerciții terapeutice, electroterapie.

###### Afecțiuni neurologice

Exemple: AVC, Parkinson, scleroză multiplă, neuropatii.

Tratament: stimulare neuromusculară, exerciții de coordonare și echilibru.

###### Afecțiuni reumatologice

Exemple: artrită, artroză, spondiloză, fibromialgie.

Tratament: electroterapie, ultrasunete, hidroterapie.

###### Afecțiuni respiratorii

Exemple: astm, bronșită cronică, BPOC.

Tratament: exerciții respiratorii, drenaj postural, masaj terapeutic.

###### Recuperare post-operatorie

Exemple: protezare de șold/genunchi, operații la coloană.

Tratament: programe individualizate de reabilitare, fizioterapie intensivă.

- 🔗 `#`

Is saline therapy safe for children?

Yes, salt therapy is considered safe for children and can be particularly beneficial for those suffering from respiratory conditions or allergies. The salty environment helps to clear the airways and reduce inflammation, which can relieve symptoms and improve children's overall health. However, it is advisable to consult your pediatrician before starting treatment to make sure it is suitable for your child.

- 🔗 `#`

Este fizioterapia dureroasă?

Fizioterapia nu ar trebui să provoace durere severă, însă unele proceduri pot provoca un ușor disconfort inițial, care dispare pe măsură ce corpul se adaptează.

- 🔗 `#`

Se poate face fizioterapie în paralel cu alte tratamente?

Da, fizioterapia poate fi combinată cu tratamente medicamentoase, kinetoterapie și alte forme de recuperare medicală.

- 🖼 imagine: `/web/image/51690-98794d9c/user-md_9856334.webp` — alt: „” — arhivat: 80bf4fc4f5594cf2bba0.webp

## `/en/ghid-intrebari-medicina-interna`

- Titlu: Ghid intrebari medicina interna | sevalia.ro
- Descriere: —

- 🖼 imagine-fundal: `/web/image/53847-4fb28f50/DSC03914.webp` — alt: „” — arhivat: a29484ccd8808fd17665.webp

- 🖼 imagine-fundal: `/web/image/53847-4fb28f50/DSC03914.webp` — alt: „” — arhivat: a29484ccd8808fd17665.webp

### Ce întrebări să îi pui medicului internist

Vrei să fii mai pregătit la următoarea vizită la medic?

### HYDRO PHYSIO - Underwater treadmill

###### The Hydro Physio underwater treadmill is a state-of-the-art piece of equipment used in physiotherapy and medical rehabilitation. It combines the benefits of physical exercise with the therapeutic properties of water, allowing movement without major joint impact. The system includes a heated water cabin and an adjustable treadmill where patients can exercise in safety and comfort.

###### Water reduces your perceived body weight by up to 80%, which means less stress on joints, ligaments and spine, facilitating recovery.

#### Rheumatic conditions

#### Post-operative recovery

#### Sports injuries

#### Sprains

#### Lumbar disc herniations

##### Increase muscle endurance without overload

﻿Book an appointment﻿

### Modern medical recovery in step with technology and p

The therapy begins with the patient entering the Hydro Physio cabin, where the water level is adjusted according to height and the condition being treated. The water is kept at a comfortable temperature (around 32-35°C), promoting muscle relaxation and stimulating circulation.

The treadmill can be set at variable speeds, and the patient walks, jogs lightly or performs specific exercises recommended by the physiotherapist. Thanks to hydrostatic pressure, movements are more fluid and the sensation of heaviness is reduced, allowing effective physical activity even for people with severe joint pain.

Throughout the session, the patient is monitored and the exercises can be adjusted in real time to suit their progress and comfort. The Hydro Physio system also allows supervised balance or stretching exercises.

This form of therapy is non-invasive, safe and perceived by most patients as relaxing and motivating.

##### RECOMMENDATIONS

The main indications of the Hydro Physio treadmill are:

Orthopedic or neurological recovery

Therapy for chronic pain

Sports rehabilitation

Locomotor rehabilitation after trauma

##### CONTRAINDICATIONS

The procedure is not recommended for patients with the following conditions:

Active skin infections or open sores

Unstable heart conditions

Uncontrolled epilepsy

Urinary/fecal incontinence

Contraindications are not absolute.

A specialist medical consultation and doctor's approval is essential.

## `/en/kinesio-taping`

- Titlu: Kinesio Taping | Susținere musculară și recuperare fără restricții
- Descriere: Kinesio Taping aplicat de specialiști în recuperare medicală. Susținere musculară, durere redusă, mișcare liberă și recuperare rapidă.

- 🖼 imagine-fundal: `/web/image/53004-b3527bf3/IMG-174.webp` — alt: „” — arhivat: 124a05dd25c80d838301.webp

### KINESIO TAPING

- 🖼 imagine-fundal: `/web/image/53005-b3527bf3/IMG-174.webp` — alt: „” — arhivat: 124a05dd25c80d838301.webp

### KINESIO TAPING

Kinesio Taping is an innovative muscle support technique, developed in the 1970s, which has since become a standard solution in medical and sports rehabilitation. The application of kinesiological taping helps to reduce pain, support joints and functionally re-educate affected segments without restricting natural movements.

The material of the bands is hypoallergenic, elastic, breathable and mimics the extensibility of human skin. Application is made according to the purpose: muscle support, postural correction, edema reduction or movement facilitation. Our therapists analyze posture, biomechanics and level of dysfunction before application to achieve maximum therapeutic effect.

Kinesio Taping can be used alone or as an integrated part of a medical rehabilitation program (alongside physiotherapy, massage, laser, etc.). It is suitable for patients of all ages, including athletes, sedentary or post-operative patients.

Results often appear within the first 24-48 hours, with a duration of action of 3-5 days after application. It is a safe, comfortable procedure with no side effects when applied correctly.

#### Sprains and joint instability

#### Tendinitis, bursitis, plantar fasciitis

#### Low back, neck or chest pain

#### Impingement syndrome

#### Lymphatic edema

Book an appointment

### Kinesiology tape therapy applied by functional rehabilitation specialists

Kinesio Taping is a modern therapeutic technique that uses special elastic bands strategically applied to affected areas to support muscles, reduce inflammation and improve function without restricting movement.

This method is 100% non-invasive and is commonly used in medical rehabilitation, orthopedics, neurology, but also in sports medicine. In our clinic, bands are applied exclusively by specialized therapists, depending on the patient's diagnosis and treatment goals.

##### RECOMMENDATIONS

The main indications of kinesiologic bands are:

Mild to moderate muscle or joint injuries

Joint instability (ankle, knee, shoulder)

Chronic back, neck or shoulder pain

Post-traumatic or post-operative edema

Correcting vicious posture

Impingement syndrome

Muscle overstrain

Tendinitis, bursitis, plantar fasciitis

##### CONTRAINDICATIONS

The procedure is not recommended for patients with the following conditions:

Skin lesions, dermatitis or eczema

Allergy to adhesives or textiles

Active thrombophlebitis

Areas with local infection

Active cancer in the area of application

Contraindications are not absolute.

A specialist medical consultation and doctor's approval is essential.

### Frequently asked questions about kinesio taping

- 🔗 `#`

Are the tapes uncomfortable or stiff?

No. They are very lightweight, elastic and don't restrict movement at all.

- 🔗 `#`

Can I shower with tape applied?

Yes, the bands are waterproof and can be worn for several days.

- 🔗 `#`

Is it effective for athletes?

Absolute - reduces the risk of injury and supports muscles during exercise.

- 🔗 `#`

How long does the effect last after application?

3 to 5 days, depending on application area and skin type.

- 🔗 `#`

How long do I have to wait between successive applications?

The strips are usually worn for 3-5 days, after which the skin should be assessed. Application can be resumed immediately if there is no irritation.

- 🔗 `#`

Can I do sport with taping?

Yes, Kinesio Taping is commonly used during physical activity - it provides support without restricting movement.

- 🔗 `#`

Can I use the strips at home without medical supervision?

Not recommended. Correct application requires anatomical and technical knowledge - misapplication can make the problem worse.

- 🔗 `#`

Is there pain or discomfort when applying the strips?

No, application is completely painless and most patients forget they are wearing them.

### PHYSIOTHERAPY

- 🔗 `#`

###### It is a form of therapy that uses a variety of physical exercises to treat acute, chronic, post-operative or post-traumatic orthopaedic and neuro-motor disorders. By combining active and passive movements, physiotherapy supports the recovery or improvement of health and mobility of affected areas.

###### Our team is specialized in numerous recovery techniques, and the most advanced devices and equipment in the field ensure optimal treatment through a quality medical act.
